xalw_content_monthly_effect = {
if = {
limit = { is_alive = yes NOT = { var:xalw_content_month_done ?= xalw_current_month_value } }
set_variable = { name = xalw_content_month_done value = xalw_current_month_value }
# Character root; monthly source is guarded independently of the caller.
save_scope_as = xalw_content_actor
if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = xalw_content_loan_periods } } set_variable = { name = xalw_content_loan_periods value = 0 } }
if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = xalw_content_loan_arrears } } set_variable = { name = xalw_content_loan_arrears value = 0 } }
if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = xalw_content_loan_instalment } } set_variable = { name = xalw_content_loan_instalment value = 0 } }
if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = xalw_content_loan_credit } } set_variable = { name = xalw_content_loan_credit value = 0 } }
if = {
limit = { OR = { var:xalw_content_loan_periods > 0 var:xalw_content_loan_arrears > 0 } }
set_variable = { name = xalw_content_loan_due value = var:xalw_content_loan_arrears }
if = {
 limit = { var:xalw_content_loan_periods > 0 }
 change_variable = { name = xalw_content_loan_due add = var:xalw_content_loan_instalment }
 change_variable = { name = xalw_content_loan_periods add = -1 }
}
set_variable = { name = xalw_content_loan_credit_used value = { value = var:xalw_content_loan_credit max = var:xalw_content_loan_due } }
change_variable = { name = xalw_content_loan_credit subtract = var:xalw_content_loan_credit_used }
change_variable = { name = xalw_content_loan_due subtract = var:xalw_content_loan_credit_used }
set_variable = { name = xalw_content_payment value = { value = gold min = 0 max = var:xalw_content_loan_due } }
add_gold = { value = var:xalw_content_payment multiply = -1 }
if = { limit = { var:xalw_content_payment < var:xalw_content_loan_due }
add_stress = 5
}
set_variable = { name = xalw_content_loan_arrears value = { value = var:xalw_content_loan_due subtract = var:xalw_content_payment min = 0 } }
}

if = {

# The first eligible calendar-month opportunity is consumed even if the roll fails.
set_variable = { name = xalw_content_loan_principal_year value = current_year }
if = { limit = { var:xalw_content_loan_periods = 0 var:xalw_content_loan_arrears = 0 }

if = { limit = { var:xalw_content_source = { var:xalw_resonance < 40 } } multiply = 0.5 }
else_if = { limit = { var:xalw_content_source = { var:xalw_resonance < 80 } } multiply = 0.75 }

if = { limit = { var:xalw_content_effective_rank > 0 var:xalw_content_source = { var:xalw_content_fickle_rank ?= 1 } } multiply = 0.75 }
if = { limit = { var:xalw_content_effective_rank > 0 var:xalw_content_source = { var:xalw_content_fickle_rank ?= 2 } } multiply = 0.5 }
if = { limit = { var:xalw_content_effective_rank > 0 var:xalw_content_source = { var:xalw_content_fickle_rank ?= 3 } } multiply = 0.25 } }
if = { limit = { var:xalw_content_effective_rank = 1 }
add_gold = 25
}
if = { limit = { var:xalw_content_effective_rank = 2 }
add_gold = 50
}
if = { limit = { var:xalw_content_effective_rank = 3 }
add_gold = 75
}

}
